# Skills · C · part 162

744 entries, cold-email-doctor to coldbox-view-rendering. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cold-email-doctor**: Read-only cold-email deliverability consultant that works across ANY platform — Smartlead, Instantly, PlusVibe, SendKit, EmailBison, ZapMail, Icemail, raw IMAP mailboxes, or plain DNS. Pull a prospect's real sending data, normalize it, grade it against deliverability best practices, and produce a clear consulting audit. Use whenever someone wants a cold-email/deliverability audit, asks "why am I landing in spam / bouncing / not getting replies", wants their sending setup reviewed, or connects any email-sending tool for analysis. Never changes anything — read-only. ([sakshamj15/cold-email-doctor](https://github.com/sakshamj15/cold-email-doctor/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **cold-email-doctrine**: Draft concise, evidence-based cold outreach using the target project's current buyer, approved offer and campaign boundaries. No embedded sender state, platform benchmark or legacy product scope. ([Opondo300/born-builders-agent-library](https://github.com/Opondo300/born-builders-agent-library/tree/HEAD/skills/cold-email-doctrine))
- **cold-email-doctrine**: Build n Bloom cold email doctrine for NDIS SIL/SDA outreach. 5-email sequence architecture (day 0/3/7/10/14), personalisation tiers (9-18% reply rates), {{personalizedLine}} generation via n8n+Claude API, spintax rules, reply handling scripts, lead scoring minimum 50pts, Instantly infrastructure rules (SPF/DKIM/warmup/bounce), key metrics. Use when writing outreach sequences, cold email copy, or configuring Instantly campaigns. ([Opondo300/born-builders-agent-library](https://github.com/Opondo300/born-builders-agent-library/tree/HEAD/archive/2026-09-27/skills/cold-email-doctrine))

- **cold-start-interview**: 首次运行访谈以建立AI治理实践配置：适用法规、 AI系统清单、红线、审批工作流和输出偏好。 在插件首次安装时自动运行。使用 --redo 可重新运行。 ([zhou210712/claude-for-legal-ZH](https://github.com/zhou210712/claude-for-legal-ZH/tree/HEAD/ai-governance-legal/skills/cold-start-interview) · ★248)
- **cold-start-interview**: 实践画像访谈，推荐并安装社区法律技能的入门包。这是整个生态系统的冷启动—— 询问你是什么类型的律师并推荐首先安装什么。在新安装、用户说"帮我开始"或 "我应该安装什么"时使用，或在添加或移除 MCP 连接器后重新运行集成可用性检查。 ([zhou210712/claude-for-legal-ZH](https://github.com/zhou210712/claude-for-legal-ZH/tree/HEAD/legal-builder-hub/skills/cold-start-interview) · ★248)
- **cold-start-interview**: 内部冷启动访谈（需求清单 + 先前备忘录），或用于逐项交易上下文的 --new-deal。模块化：识别哪些实务领域适用（并购、董事会与公司秘书、 公众公司、主体管理），然后对每个活跃模块询问有针对性的问题， 仅将相关章节写入插件配置。在全新安装时、CLAUDE.md 仍有 [PLACEHOLDER] 标记时、开始新交易时、或重新检查集成或刷新某一模块时使用。 ([zhou210712/claude-for-legal-ZH](https://github.com/zhou210712/claude-for-legal-ZH/tree/HEAD/corporate-legal/skills/cold-start-interview) · ★248)

- **cold-start-interview**: 冷启动访谈——连接您的上线追踪器、读取过往审查记录、学习您的风险校准。 在全新安装、产品法务入职或插件配置含有占位符时使用。运行 --redo 重新访谈， 或 --check-integrations 仅重新检测连接器。 ([zhou210712/claude-for-legal-ZH](https://github.com/zhou210712/claude-for-legal-ZH/tree/HEAD/product-legal/skills/cold-start-interview) · ★248)
